		2:
			current_stat_type = "total_damage"
	
	await _refresh_leaderboard()  # ← await добавлен

func _on_friends_updated() -> void:
	_refresh_friends_list()

func _on_request_received(request_data: Dictionary) -> void:
	_refresh_requests_list()

func _on_gift_received(gift_data: Dictionary) -> void:
	_refresh_gifts_list()

func _on_online_status_changed(status_dict: Dictionary) -> void:
	_refresh_friends_list()

func _on_friends_list_item_clicked(index: int, at_position: Vector2, mouse_button_index: int) -> void:
	if mouse_button_index != MOUSE_BUTTON_RIGHT:
		return
	
	var friend = friends_list.get_item_metadata(index)
	if not friend:
		return
	
	var menu = load("res://Scenes/UI/FriendActionMenu.tscn").instantiate()
	menu.friend_data = friend
	add_child(menu)

func _on_gifts_list_item_clicked(index: int, at_position: Vector2, mouse_button_index: int) -> void:
	if mouse_button_index != MOUSE_BUTTON_LEFT:
		return
	
	var gift = gifts_list.get_item_metadata(index)
	if not gift:
		return
	
	FriendsService.claim_gift(index)


# Scenes\UI\friend_action_menu.gd
extends ColorRect

@onready var lbl_nickname: Label = $Panel/VBoxContainer/LblNickname
@onready var lbl_code: Label = $Panel/VBoxContainer/LblCode
@onready var btn_send_gift: Button = $Panel/VBoxContainer/BtnSendGift
@onready var btn_copy_deck: Button = $Panel/VBoxContainer/BtnCopyDeck
@onready var btn_view_profile: Button = $Panel/VBoxContainer/BtnViewProfile
@onready var btn_remove: Button = $Panel/VBoxContainer/BtnRemove
@onready var btn_close: Button = $Panel/VBoxContainer/BtnClose

var friend_data: Dictionary = {}

func _ready() -> void:
	_connect_signals()
	_populate_data()
	_check_buttons_availability()

func _connect_signals() -> void:
	btn_send_gift.pressed.connect(_on_send_gift_pressed)
	btn_copy_deck.pressed.connect(_on_copy_deck_pressed)
	btn_view_profile.pressed.connect(_on_view_profile_pressed)
	btn_remove.pressed.connect(_on_remove_pressed)
	btn_close.pressed.connect(_on_close_pressed)

func _populate_data() -> void:
	if friend_data.is_empty():
		return
	
	lbl_nickname.text = friend_data.get("friend_nickname", "Unknown")
	lbl_code.text = friend_data.get("friend_code", "#00000000")

func _check_buttons_availability() -> void:
	var friend_uid = friend_data.get("friend_uid", "")
	
	# Проверка доступности подарка
	var can_send = FriendsService.can_send_gift_to(friend_uid)
	btn_send_gift.disabled = not can_send
	
	if not can_send:
		var days_left = FriendsService.get_gift_cooldown_days(friend_uid)
		btn_send_gift.text = " Подарок через %d дн." % days_left
	
	# Проверка доступности копирования колоды
	var can_copy = FriendsService.can_copy_friend_deck(friend_uid)
	btn_copy_deck.disabled = not can_copy
	
	if not can_copy:
		btn_copy_deck.text = "📋 Колода недоступна (нет башен)"

func _on_send_gift_pressed() -> void:
	var friend_uid = friend_data.get("friend_uid", "")
	FriendsService.send_gift(friend_uid, "gold")
	queue_free()

func _on_copy_deck_pressed() -> void:
	var friend_uid = friend_data.get("friend_uid", "")
	FriendsService.copy_friend_deck(friend_uid)
	queue_free()

func _on_view_profile_pressed() -> void:
	# Открытие профиля друга
	var profile_screen = load("res://Scenes/UI/ProfileScreen.tscn").instantiate()
	get_parent().add_child(profile_screen)
	queue_free()

func _on_remove_pressed() -> void:
	var friend_uid = friend_data.get("friend_uid", "")
	FriendsService.remove_friend(friend_uid)
	queue_free()

func _on_close_pressed() -> void:
	queue_free()


# Scenes\UI\KeybindRow.gd
# Scenes/UI/KeybindRow.gd
extends HBoxContainer

signal key_changed(action, new_key)

@onready var label_action = $LabelAction
@onready var button_key = $ButtonKey

var action_id = ""
var current_key = KEY_NONE
var is_waiting = false

func setup(action: String, key: Key):
	action_id = action
	current_key = key
	
	# Локализация имени действия
	label_action.text = tr("KEY_ACTION_" + action.to_upper())
	_update_button_text()

func _update_button_text():
	button_key.text = OS.get_keycode_string(current_key)

func _on_button_key_pressed():
	if is_waiting: return
	is_waiting = true
	button_key.text = tr("KEY_PRESS_KEY")

func _input(event):
	if not is_waiting: return
	
	if event is InputEventKey and event.pressed:
		var new_key = event.keycode
		
		# Проверка на конфликт через SettingsManager
		if SettingsManager.set_keybind(action_id, new_key):
			current_key = new_key
			_update_button_text()
			emit_signal("key_changed", action_id, new_key)
			is_waiting = false
		else:
			button_key.text = tr("KEY_CONFLICT")
			await get_tree().create_timer(1.0).timeout
			_update_button_text()
			is_waiting = false
		
		get_viewport().set_input_as_handled()

# Scenes\UI\loader.gd
# Loader.gd
extends Node

func _ready():
	# Ждем первого кадра отрисовки
	await get_tree().process_frame
	await get_tree().process_frame
	
	_load_correct_menu()

func _load_correct_menu():
	var scene_path = "res://Scenes/UI/Menu.tscn"
	
	if OS.get_name() == "Android" or OS.get_name() == "iOS":
		scene_path = "res://Scenes/Mobile/Menu_mobile.tscn"
	
	# Безопасная смена сцены
	call_deferred("_change_scene_safe", scene_path)

func _change_scene_safe(path):
	get_tree().change_scene_to_file(path)


# Scenes\UI\login_screen.gd
extends Control

@onready var btn_google: Button = $Panel/VBoxContainer/BtnGoogle
@onready var btn_email: Button = $Panel/VBoxContainer/BtnEmail
@onready var btn_guest: Button = $Panel/VBoxContainer/BtnGuest
@onready var status_label: Label = $Panel/VBoxContainer/StatusLabel
@onready var loading_spinner: TextureRect = $Panel/VBoxContainer/LoadingSpinner

var is_loading := false
var email_dialog = null

func _ready() -> void:
	print("\n=== LOGIN SCREEN DEBUG ===")
	print("AuthService exists: ", AuthService != null)
	
	var firebase = get_node_or_null("/root/Firebase")
	print("Firebase exists: ", firebase != null)
	
	if firebase:
		print("Firebase.Auth exists: ", firebase.get_node_or_null("Auth") != null)
		print("Firebase.Firestore exists: ", firebase.get_node_or_null("Firestore") != null)
	
	if AuthService:
		print("Firebase ready: ", AuthService.is_firebase_ready())
	
	_connect_signals()
	_show_buttons(true)

func _connect_signals() -> void:
	btn_google.pressed.connect(_on_google_pressed)
	btn_email.pressed.connect(_on_email_pressed)
	btn_guest.pressed.connect(_on_guest_pressed)
	
	# === ИСПРАВЛЕНО: Подключаем сигналы AuthService ===
	if AuthService:
		AuthService.auth_completed.connect(_on_auth_completed)
		AuthService.auth_failed.connect(_on_auth_failed)
		AuthService.profile_created.connect(_on_profile_created)

func _show_buttons(show: bool) -> void:
	btn_google.visible = show
	btn_google.disabled = not show
	btn_email.visible = show
	btn_email.disabled = not show
	btn_guest.visible = show
	btn_guest.disabled = not show

func _on_google_pressed() -> void:
	if is_loading:
		return
	
	AuthService.sign_in_with_google()
	_set_loading(true)
	_set_status("Вход через Google...")

func _on_email_pressed() -> void:
	if is_loading:
		return
	
	# Создаём диалог входа
	email_dialog = load("res://Scenes/UI/EmailLoginDialog.tscn").instantiate()
	add_child(email_dialog)
	
	email_dialog.login_requested.connect(_on_login_requested)
	email_dialog.register_requested.connect(_on_register_requested)

func _on_login_requested(email: String, password: String) -> void:
	if email_dialog:
		email_dialog.set_status("Вход...")
	AuthService.sign_in_with_email(email, password)
	_set_loading(true)

func _on_register_requested(email: String, password: String, nickname: String) -> void:
	if email_dialog:
		email_dialog.set_status("Регистрация...")
	AuthService.register_with_email(email, password, nickname)
	_set_loading(true)

func _on_guest_pressed() -> void:
	if is_loading:
		return
	AuthService.sign_in_as_guest()
	_set_loading(true)
	_set_status("Вход как гость...")
	get_tree().change_scene_to_file("res://Scenes/UI/Menu.tscn")

func _on_auth_completed(success: bool, user_data: Dictionary) -> void:
	_set_loading(false)
	
	if email_dialog:
		email_dialog.queue_free()
		email_dialog = null
	
	if success:
		_set_status("Успешный вход!")
		await get_tree().create_timer(0.5).timeout
		
		if OS.get_name() == "Android" or OS.get_name() == "iOS":
			get_tree().change_scene_to_file("res://Scenes/Mobile/Menu_mobile.tscn")
		else:
			get_tree().change_scene_to_file("res://Scenes/UI/Menu.tscn")
	else:
		_set_status("Ошибка входа")
		_show_buttons(true)

func _on_auth_failed(error_message: String) -> void:
	_set_loading(false)
	
	if email_dialog:
		email_dialog.set_status(error_message)
	else:
		_set_status(error_message)
		_show_buttons(true)

func _on_profile_created() -> void:
	_set_status("Профиль создан!")

func _set_status(text: String) -> void:
	if is_instance_valid(status_label):
		status_label.text = text

func _set_loading(show: bool) -> void:
	is_loading = show
	if is_instance_valid(loading_spinner):
		loading_spinner.visible = show
	_show_buttons(not show)

func _navigate_to_main_menu() -> void:
	var main_menu = load("res://Scenes/UI/Menu.tscn").instantiate()
	get_tree().current_scene.add_child(main_menu)
	queue_free()


# Scenes\UI\Menu.gd
extends Control

# --- UI References ---
@onready var money_label = %MoneyLabel
@onready var crit_label = %CritLabel
@onready var nickname_label = %NicknameLabel

@onready var but_new_game = %ButtonNewGame
@onready var but_shop = %ButtonShop
@onready var but_settings = %ButtonSettings
@onready var but_promotion = %ButtonPromotion
@onready var but_exit = %ButtonExit
@onready var but_task = %ButtonTask
@onready var but_reward = %ButtonReward

# Auth Buttons (Top Left)
@onready var but_profile_icon = %ButtonProfileIcon
@onready var but_logout_icon = %ButtonLogoutIcon
@onready var but_friends_icon = %ButtonFriendsIcon

@onready var loading_panel = %MenuLoadingPanel
@onready var bg_generator = $BackgroundGenerator

# Badges
@onready var promotion_badge = but_promotion.get_node_or_null("NinePatchRect")
@onready var task_badge = but_task.get_node_or_null("NinePatchRect/NinePatchRect")

var _firebase_initialized := false
var _login_button = null

# Пути к ассетам фона (Menu0-Menu7.png)
var _menu_asset_paths = [
	"res://Assets/Icons/Menu/Menu0.png",
	"res://Assets/Icons/Menu/Menu1.png",
	"res://Assets/Icons/Menu/Menu2.png",
	"res://Assets/Icons/Menu/Menu3.png",
	"res://Assets/Icons/Menu/Menu4.png",
	"res://Assets/Icons/Menu/Menu5.png",
	"res://Assets/Icons/Menu/Menu6.png",
	"res://Assets/Icons/Menu/Menu7.png"
]

func _ready():
	# === СКРЫВАЕМ ВСЕ КНОПКИ И ПОКАЗЫВАЕМ ЗАГРУЗКУ ===
	_hide_all_buttons()
	_show_loading(true)
	
	TranslationServer.set_locale(DataManager.data.get("SettingsGame", {}).get("language", "en"))
	
	if OS.get_name() != "Android" and OS.get_name() != "iOS":
		DisplayServer.window_set_size(
			Vector2i(
				DataManager.data.get("SettingsGame", {}).get("width", 1600),
				DataManager.data.get("SettingsGame", {}).get("height", 900)
			)
		)
	
	# Generate Random Background
	_generate_background_decor()
	
	# Setup hover effects for all buttons
	_setup_button_hover_effects()
	
	if AuthService:
		AuthService.auth_completed.connect(_on_auth_state_changed)
	
	# Connect Signals
	if but_new_game: but_new_game.pressed.connect(on_new_game_pressed)
	if but_shop: but_shop.pressed.connect(shop)
	if but_settings: but_settings.pressed.connect(settings)
	if but_promotion: but_promotion.pressed.connect(promotion)
	if but_exit: but_exit.pressed.connect(on_quit_pressed)
	if but_task: but_task.pressed.connect(task)
	if but_reward: but_reward.pressed.connect(reward)
	
	# Top Bar Auth Buttons
	if but_profile_icon: but_profile_icon.pressed.connect(profile)
	if but_logout_icon: but_logout_icon.pressed.connect(logout)
	if but_friends_icon: but_friends_icon.pressed.connect(friends)
	
	if promotion_badge: promotion_badge.visible = check_promotion()
	if task_badge: task_badge.visible = check_task()
	
	# === ЖДЁМ FIREBASE И AUTH ===
	await _wait_for_firebase()
	await _wait_for_auth_initialization()
	
	# === ПРОВЕРЯЕМ АВТОРИЗАЦИЮ И ПОКАЗЫВАЕМ UI ===
	if AuthService and AuthService.is_authenticated:
		print("[Menu] ✅ Пользователь авторизован")
		await _load_profile_from_firebase()
		_show_logged_in_ui()
	else:
		print("[Menu] ⚠️ Пользователь не авторизован")
		_show_logged_out_ui()
	
	_update_money_ui()
	
	# === СКРЫВАЕМ ЗАГРУЗКУ ===
	_show_loading(false)

func _setup_button_hover_effects():
	"""Настраивает hover эффекты для всех кнопок с tooltip"""
	var all_buttons = [
		but_new_game, but_shop, but_settings, but_promotion, but_exit,
		but_profile_icon, but_friends_icon, but_logout_icon, but_task, but_reward
	]
	
	for btn in all_buttons:
		if btn and btn.has_node("Tooltip"):
			var tooltip = btn.get_node("Tooltip")
			btn.mouse_entered.connect(_on_button_hover.bind(btn, tooltip, true))
			btn.mouse_exited.connect(_on_button_hover.bind(btn, tooltip, false))

func _on_button_hover(btn: Button, tooltip: Label, is_hovering: bool):
	"""Показывает/скрывает tooltip при наведении"""
	if tooltip:
		tooltip.visible = is_hovering
	
	# Эффект масштабирования при наведении
	if is_hovering:
		var tween = create_tween()
		tween.tween_property(btn, "scale", Vector2(1.1, 1.1), 0.15)
	else:
		var tween = create_tween()
		tween.tween_property(btn, "scale", Vector2(1.0, 1.0), 0.15)

func _generate_background_decor():
	"""Создает случайные деревья и камни на фоне без наложений"""
	if not bg_generator: return
	
	var rng = RandomNumberGenerator.new()
	rng.randomize()
	
	var screen_size = get_viewport_rect().size
	
	# Отступы от UI элементов
	var margin_left = 50
	var margin_right = 330  # Отступ справа (под Дорогу Славы если будет)
	var margin_top = 100
	var margin_bottom = 100
	
	# Минимальное расстояние между объектами (чтобы не накладывались)
	var min_distance = 70
	
	# Список уже размещённых позиций для проверки наложений
	var placed_positions = []
	
	# Максимум попыток разместить объект (чтобы не зависло если места мало)
	var max_attempts = 100
	
	# Генерируем 40 объектов
	for i in range(40):
		var decor = TextureRect.new()
		
		# Случайный выбор ассета из Menu0-Menu7
		var rand_index = rng.randi_range(0, _menu_asset_paths.size() - 1)
		var texture_path = _menu_asset_paths[rand_index]
		
		# Загружаем текстуру
		if ResourceLoader.exists(texture_path):
			decor.texture = load(texture_path)
		else:
			decor.texture = load("res://Assets/Icons/circle.png")
		
		# === ВАЖНО: Сохраняем пропорции изображения ===
		decor.stretch_mode = TextureRect.STRETCH_KEEP_CENTERED
		decor.expand_mode = TextureRect.EXPAND_IGNORE_SIZE
		
		
		# === Пытаемся найти позицию без наложений ===
		var position_found = false
		var attempts = 0
		
		while not position_found and attempts < max_attempts:
			attempts += 1
			
			# Случайная позиция в допустимых границах
			var x = rng.randf_range(margin_left, screen_size.x - margin_right - 50)
			var y = rng.randf_range(margin_top, screen_size.y - margin_bottom - 50)
			var new_pos = Vector2(x, y)
			
			# Проверяем расстояние до всех уже размещённых объектов
			var too_close = false
			for placed_pos in placed_positions:
				var distance = new_pos.distance_to(placed_pos)
				if distance < min_distance:
					too_close = true
					break
			
			if not too_close:
				decor.position = new_pos
				placed_positions.append(new_pos)
				position_found = true
		
		# Если не нашли место после макс попыток - пропускаем этот объект
		if not position_found:
			continue
		
		# Случайная прозрачность для глубины
		decor.modulate.a = rng.randf_range(0.5, 0.9)
		decor.z_index = -1
		
		bg_generator.add_child(decor)
		
func _wait_for_auth_initialization() -> void:
	if not AuthService: return
	var timeout = 5.0
	var step = 0.1
	while timeout > 0 and not AuthService.is_initialization_complete():
		await get_tree().create_timer(step).timeout
		timeout -= step
		
func _hide_all_buttons():
	"""Скрывает ВСЕ кнопки (используется только при загрузке и смене состояния авторизации)"""
	var buttons = [
		but_new_game, but_shop, but_settings, but_promotion, but_exit, 
		but_task, but_reward, but_profile_icon, but_logout_icon, but_friends_icon
	]
	for btn in buttons:
		if btn: btn.visible = false
	if _login_button: _login_button.visible = false

func friends():
	var friends_screen = load("res://Scenes/UI/FriendsScreen.tscn").instantiate()
	get_tree().current_scene.add_child(friends_screen)

func _wait_for_firebase() -> void:
	var timeout = 5.0
	var step = 0.1
	while timeout > 0:
		await get_tree().create_timer(step).timeout
		timeout -= step
		if AuthService and AuthService.is_firebase_ready():
			_firebase_initialized = true
			break

func _show_loading(show: bool):
	"""Показать/скрыть панель загрузки"""
	if loading_panel:
		loading_panel.visible = show

func _show_logged_in_ui():
	"""Показать UI для авторизованного (Профиль, Друзья, Выход)"""
	_hide_all_buttons()
	
	# Top Bar Left - показываем профиль, друзья, выход
	if but_profile_icon: but_profile_icon.visible = true
	if but_friends_icon: but_friends_icon.visible = true
	if but_logout_icon: but_logout_icon.visible = true
	
	# Center Dock - все кнопки меню
	if but_new_game: but_new_game.visible = true
	if but_shop: but_shop.visible = true
	if but_settings: but_settings.visible = true
	if but_exit: but_exit.visible = true
	if but_task: but_task.visible = true
	if but_promotion: but_promotion.visible = true
	if but_reward: but_reward.visible = true

func _show_logged_out_ui():
	"""Показать UI для гостя (Кнопка Войти вместо Профиля/Друзей)"""
	_hide_all_buttons()
	
	# Top Bar Left - скрываем профиль/друзья/выход
	if but_profile_icon: but_profile_icon.visible = false
	if but_friends_icon: but_friends_icon.visible = false
	if but_logout_icon: but_logout_icon.visible = false
	
	# Создаём кнопку входа если её нет
	if not _login_button:
		_login_button = Button.new()
		_login_button.name = "ButtonLoginIcon"
		_login_button.unique_name_in_owner = true
		_login_button.custom_minimum_size = Vector2(100, 44)
		_login_button.pressed.connect(login)
		
		# StyleBox через set() - ПРАВИЛЬНО
		_login_button.set("theme_override_styles/normal", StyleBoxEmpty.new())
		_login_button.set("theme_override_styles/hover", StyleBoxEmpty.new())
		
		var label = Label.new()
		label.text = "KEY_ENTRANCE"
		label.horizontal_alignment = HORIZONTAL_ALIGNMENT_CENTER
		label.vertical_alignment = VERTICAL_ALIGNMENT_CENTER
		label.set_anchors_preset(Control.PRESET_FULL_RECT)
		label.set("theme_override_font_sizes/font_size", 16)
		label.set("theme_override_colors/font_color", Color(0.937255, 0.65098, 0.54902, 1))
		_login_button.add_child(label)
		
		get_node("TopBar/TopBar_HBoxContainer/TopBar_LeftGroup").add_child(_login_button)
		
		# Setup hover effect for login button
		var tooltip = Label.new()
		tooltip.name = "Tooltip"
		tooltip.visible = false
		tooltip.text = "KEY_ENTRANCE"
		tooltip.horizontal_alignment = HORIZONTAL_ALIGNMENT_CENTER
		tooltip.set("theme_override_colors/font_color", Color(1, 1, 1, 1))
		tooltip.set("theme_override_font_sizes/font_size", 12)
		tooltip.set_anchors_preset(Control.PRESET_FULL_RECT)
		tooltip.offset_top = -35
		tooltip.offset_bottom = -10
		_login_button.add_child(tooltip)
		
		_login_button.mouse_entered.connect(_on_button_hover.bind(_login_button, tooltip, true))
		_login_button.mouse_exited.connect(_on_button_hover.bind(_login_button, tooltip, false))
	
	if _login_button: _login_button.visible = true
	
	# Center Dock - все кнопки меню доступны и гостям
	if but_new_game: but_new_game.visible = true
	if but_shop: but_shop.visible = true
	if but_settings: but_settings.visible = true
	if but_exit: but_exit.visible = true
	if but_task: but_task.visible = true
	if but_promotion: but_promotion.visible = true
	if but_reward: but_reward.visible = true

func _on_auth_state_changed(success: bool, user_data: Dictionary):
	"""Обновить UI при входе/выходе"""
	if success:
		_hide_all_buttons()  # Скрываем пока загружаем профиль
		await _load_profile_from_firebase()
		_show_logged_in_ui()
	else:
		_show_logged_out_ui()
	_update_money_ui()

func _load_profile_from_firebase() -> void:
	"""Загружает профиль из Firebase и обновляет локальный кэш"""
	if not AuthService or not AuthService.is_authenticated: return
	
	var firebase = get_node_or_null("/root/Firebase")
	var firestore = firebase.get_node_or_null("Firestore") if firebase else null
	
	if not firestore: return
	
	var uid = AuthService.get_current_uid()
	var collection = firestore.collection("users")
	var doc_data = await collection.get_doc(uid)
	
	if doc_data and doc_data.has_method("get_unsafe_document"):
		var profile_dict = doc_data.get_unsafe_document()
		
		if not profile_dict.is_empty():
			print("[Menu] ✅ Профиль загружен из Firebase: ", profile_dict.get("nickname", "Unknown"))
			print("[Menu] 💰 Золото: ", profile_dict.get("gold", 0))
			
			FirebaseHelper.save_offline_profile_data(profile_dict)
			OfflineManager.cache_profile(profile_dict)
			AuthService.current_user["profile"] = profile_dict
		else:
			print("[Menu] ⚠️ Профиль пуст")
	else:
		print("[Menu] ❌ Не удалось получить профиль из Firestore")

func _update_money_ui():
	"""Обновить данные профиля в UI"""
	if AuthService:
		var profile = AuthService.get_profile_data()
		if money_label:
			money_label.text = str(profile.get("gold", DataManager.data_money))
		if crit_label:
			crit_label.text = str(DataManager.critical_damage)
		
		# Обновляем никнейм
		if nickname_label:
			var nick = profile.get("nickname", "Guest")
			if nick == "" or nick == "Guest": nick = AuthService.get_current_nickname()
			nickname_label.text = nick

func on_new_game_pressed():
	UiManager.menu_object = load("res://Scenes/SupportScenes/choose_game_mode.tscn").instantiate()

		_show_logged_out_ui()
		_update_money_ui()
		print("[Menu] ✅ Пользователь вышел из аккаунта")

	UiManager.menu_object = load("res://Scenes/SupportScenes/daily_tasks.tscn").instantiate()
	_hide_main_ui()

	# Логика кнопки подарка

	get_tree().quit()

func show_critical_damage():
	if crit_label: crit_label.text = str(DataManager.critical_damage)

func check_promotion():
	for i in range(len(DataManager.promotion_progress_level)):
		if int(DataManager.promotion_progress_level[i]) != 0:
			if len(str(DataManager.promotion_progress_level_data_end[i][int(DataManager.promotion_progress_level[i]) - 1])) < 4:
				return true
	return false
	
func check_task():
	for i in range(len(TasksManager.list_tasks_you)):
		if int(TasksManager.list_tasks_you[i][2]) >= int(TasksManager.list_tasks_you[i][1]):
			return true
	return false

func _hide_main_ui():
	"""Скрыть главное меню при открытии модалок"""
	if has_node("CenterDock"): get_node("CenterDock").visible = false
	if has_node("TopBar"): get_node("TopBar").visible = false
	if has_node("Reward"): get_node("Reward").visible = false


# Scenes\UI\MenuSettings.gd
# Scenes/UI/MenuSettings.gd
extends Control

@onready var dropExpansion = $MarginContainer/Panel/MarginContainer/VBoxContainer/HBoxContainer/VBoxContainer2/OptionButton
@onready var dropLanguage = $MarginContainer/Panel/MarginContainer/VBoxContainer/HBoxContainer/VBoxContainer2/OptionButton2
@onready var dropFPS = $MarginContainer/Panel/MarginContainer/VBoxContainer/HBoxContainer/VBoxContainer2/OptionButton3
@onready var keybinds_container = $MarginContainer/Panel/MarginContainer/VBoxContainer/KeybindsScroll/KeybindsList

const expansion = ["1024*546", "1280*720", "1600*900", "1920*1080"]
const language = ["en", "ru"]
const fps_options = [30, 60, 120, 0] # 0 = unlimited

const KEYBIND_ROW_SCENE = preload("res://Scenes/UI/KeybindRow.tscn")

func _ready() -> void:
	add_items()
	_setup_keybindings()

func add_items() -> void:
	# Разрешение
	dropExpansion.clear()
	for resolution in expansion:
		dropExpansion.add_item(resolution)
	dropExpansion.select(int(DataManager.data.get("SettingsGame", {}).get("current_width_height", "2")))
	
	# Язык
	dropLanguage.select(int(DataManager.data.get("SettingsGame", {}).get("current_language", "1")))
	
	# FPS
	dropFPS.clear()
	dropFPS.add_item(tr("KEY_FPS_30"))
	dropFPS.add_item(tr("KEY_FPS_60"))
	dropFPS.add_item(tr("KEY_FPS_120"))
	dropFPS.add_item(tr("KEY_FPS_UNLIMITED"))
	
	var current_fps = SettingsManager.settings.graphics.fps_limit
	match current_fps:
		30: dropFPS.select(0)
		60: dropFPS.select(1)
		120: dropFPS.select(2)
		0: dropFPS.select(3)

func _setup_keybindings():
	for child in keybinds_container.get_children():
		child.queue_free()
	
	var bindings = SettingsManager.settings.keybindings
	var order = ["build_tower_1", "build_tower_2", "build_tower_3", "build_tower_4", "cancel", "pause", "speed_up", "show_stats", "show_ranges"]
	
	for action_id in order:
		if bindings.has(action_id):
			var row = KEYBIND_ROW_SCENE.instantiate()
			keybinds_container.add_child(row)
			row.setup(action_id, bindings[action_id])

func _on_option_button_item_selected(index: int) -> void:
	var expansions = expansion[index].split("*")
	DisplayServer.window_set_size(Vector2i(int(expansions[0]), int(expansions[1])))
	DataManager.data["SettingsGame"]["current_width_height"] = str(index)
	DataManager.write_file()

func _on_option_button_2_item_selected(index: int) -> void:
	TranslationServer.set_locale(language[index])
	DataManager.data["SettingsGame"]["current_language"] = str(index)
	DataManager.write_file()
	add_items() # Обновить тексты
	_setup_keybindings()

func _on_option_button_3_item_selected(index: int) -> void:
	SettingsManager.set_fps_limit(fps_options[index])

func _on_button_back_pressed() -> void:
	var scene = "res://Scenes/UI/Menu.tscn"
	if OS.get_name() in ["Android", "iOS"]:
		scene = "res://Scenes/Mobile/Menu_mobile.tscn"
	get_tree().change_scene_to_file(scene)

func _on_button_reset_pressed() -> void:
	# Сброс всего
	SettingsManager.settings.graphics.fps_limit = 60
	SettingsManager.settings.keybindings = {
		"build_tower_1": KEY_1, "build_tower_2": KEY_2, "build_tower_3": KEY_3, "build_tower_4": KEY_4,
		"cancel": KEY_ESCAPE, "pause": KEY_SPACE, "speed_up": KEY_E, "show_stats": KEY_TAB, "show_ranges": KEY_D
	}
	SettingsManager.save_settings()
	SettingsManager.apply_settings()
	
	TranslationServer.set_locale("ru")
	DisplayServer.window_set_size(Vector2i(1600, 900))
	
	add_items()
	_setup_keybindings()


# Scenes\UI\nickname_dialog.gd
# Scenes/UI/NicknameDialog.gd
extends ColorRect
@onready var nickname_edit: LineEdit = $Panel/VBoxContainer/NicknameEdit
@onready var btn_create: Button = $Panel/VBoxContainer/BtnCreate
@onready var btn_close: Button = $Panel/VBoxContainer/BtnClose
@onready var status_label: Label = $Panel/VBoxContainer/StatusLabel

var email: String = ""
var password: String = ""

func _ready() -> void:
	_connect_signals()
	if AuthService:
		var profile = AuthService.get_profile_data()
		var nickname = profile.get("nickname", "Player")
		if nickname is Dictionary:
			nickname = str(nickname.get("stringValue", "Player"))
		nickname_edit.text = str(nickname)

func _connect_signals() -> void:
	btn_create.pressed.connect(_on_create_pressed)
	btn_close.pressed.connect(_on_close_pressed)

func _on_create_pressed() -> void:
	var nickname = nickname_edit.text.strip_edges()
	
	if nickname.length() < 3 or nickname.length() > 16:
		_set_status("Никнейм должен быть от 3 до 16 символов")
		return
	
	if ProfileManager:
		ProfileManager.change_nickname(nickname)
		_set_status("Никнейм изменён!")
		
		await _sync_nickname_to_firebase(nickname)
		
		# === ОБНОВЛЯЕМ UI ПРОФИЛЯ ===
		_refresh_profile_ui()
		
		await get_tree().create_timer(0.5).timeout
		queue_free()
	else:
		_set_status("Ошибка: ProfileManager не найден")

func _sync_nickname_to_firebase(nickname: String) -> void:
	if not AuthService or not AuthService.is_authenticated:
		return
	
	var firebase = get_node_or_null("/root/Firebase")
	var firestore = firebase.get_node_or_null("Firestore") if firebase else null
	
	if not firestore:
		return
	
	var uid = AuthService.get_current_uid()
	var collection = firestore.collection("users")
	var profile = AuthService.get_profile_data()
	
	profile["nickname"] = nickname
	
	var result = await collection.set_doc(uid, profile)
	
	FirebaseHelper.save_offline_profile_data(profile)
	OfflineManager.cache_profile(profile)
	
func _refresh_profile_ui() -> void:
	var profile_screen = get_tree().current_scene.get_parent().get_node("ProfileScreen")
	profile_screen._load_profile_data()

func _on_close_pressed() -> void:
	queue_free()

func _set_status(text: String) -> void:
	status_label.text = text


# Scenes\UI\player_container.gd
# Scenes/UI/player_container.gd
extends Control

@onready var map_node = load("res://Scenes/Maps/map_battle_0.tscn").instantiate()

var build_controller
var wave_controller
var gift_controller
var ui_controller
var game_end_controller
var health_controller

var is_player := false
var pvp_manager: Node
var spectator_enemies = {} 
var spectator_towers_map = {} 
var spectator_effects = {} 

var local_road_coords = []

# Статистика
var tower_damage_history = {} 
var enemy_kill_history = {}   

# Для MVP
var max_damage_tower_id = -1
var max_damage_val = 0

func _ready():
	map_node.name = "Map"
	add_child(map_node)
	move_child(map_node, 0)
	_initialize_controllers()
	call_deferred("_init_local_road_coords")
	
	if OS.get_name() == "Android" or OS.get_name() == "iOS":
		await get_tree().process_frame
		
		var cont_w = self.size.x
		var cont_h = self.size.y
		var map_w = 800.0 
		var map_h = 900.0 
		
		var scale_x = cont_w / map_w
		var scale_y = cont_h / map_h
		var final_scale = max(scale_x, scale_y)
		
		map_node.scale = Vector2(final_scale, final_scale)
		
		var new_w = map_w * final_scale
		var new_h = map_h * final_scale
		map_node.position.x = (cont_w - new_w) / 2.0
		map_node.position.y = (cont_h - new_h) / 2.0
	
	# === КРИТИЧЕСКОЕ ИСПРАВЛЕНИЕ: МЕЧАЕМ КОНТЕЙНЕР ===
	if is_player:
		set_meta("is_ai_player", false)
		set_meta("player_money", 500.0)
		print("[PlayerContainer] ✅ Игрок контейнер инициализирован | Деньги: 500.0")
	else:
		set_meta("is_ai_player", true)
		set_meta("ai_money", 500.0)
		print("[PlayerContainer] ✅ AI контейнер инициализирован | Деньги: 500.0")
		
func _init_local_road_coords():
	if is_instance_valid(map_node) and map_node.has_node("TowerExlusion"):
		local_road_coords = GameManager.get_matching_tile_coords(map_node.get_node("TowerExlusion"))

func set_as_player(manager: Node):
	is_player = true
	pvp_manager = manager
	GameSession.current_money_in_game_session = 500.0
	GameSession.base_health = 100
	GameSession.current_wave = 0 
	set_meta("is_ai_player", false)
	if ui_controller: 
		ui_controller.initialize(self, true)
	
	print("[PlayerContainer] ✅ Игрок инициализирован | Деньги: 500 | HP: 100")

func set_as_ai_player():
	is_player = false
	
	# Инициализируем meta для AI
	set_meta("is_ai_player", true)
	set_meta("ai_money", 500.0)
	set_meta("ai_hp", 100)
	
	if ui_controller: 
		ui_controller.initialize(self, false)
	
	print("[PlayerContainer] ✅ AI инициализирован | Деньги: 500 (скрыты) | HP: 100 (виден)")
	
func start_game(wave_data: Array):
	if wave_controller:
		wave_controller.set_wave_data(wave_data)
		if is_player: wave_controller.start_next_wave()

func _initialize_controllers():
	var controllers_container = Node.new()
	controllers_container.name = "Controllers"
	add_child(controllers_container)
	build_controller = _create_controller("res://Controllers/BuildController.gd", controllers_container)
	wave_controller = _create_controller("res://Controllers/WaveController.gd", controllers_container)
	gift_controller = _create_controller("res://Controllers/GiftController.gd", controllers_container)
	ui_controller = _create_controller("res://Controllers/UIController.gd", controllers_container)
	game_end_controller = _create_controller("res://Controllers/GameEndController.gd", controllers_container)
	health_controller = _create_controller("res://Controllers/HealthController.gd", controllers_container)
	build_controller.initialize(self)
	wave_controller.initialize(self)
	gift_controller.initialize(self)
	ui_controller.initialize(self, false) 
	game_end_controller.initialize(self)
	health_controller.initialize(self)
	GameManager.get_road_coords(self)

func _create_controller(script_path: String, parent_node: Node) -> Node:
	var script = load(script_path)
	var controller = Node.new()
	controller.set_script(script)
	parent_node.add_child(controller)
	return controller

func update_opponent_hp(new_hp: int):
	"""Обновляет HP противника в UI (для отображения HP врага)"""
	get_node("UI/HUD/InfoBar/H2/HP").text = str(new_hp)

func on_base_damage(damage: int):
	"""Обработка урона по базе контейнера"""
	
	print("\n[PlayerContainer] ⚔️ on_base_damage вызван")
	print("  Контейнер: ", name)
	print("  Урон: ", damage)
	
	# 1. Определяем тип контейнера
	var is_ai_container = has_meta("is_ai_player") and get_meta("is_ai_player")
	print("  is_ai_container: ", is_ai_container)
	
	if is_ai_container:
		# === AI КОНТЕЙНЕР ===
		var ai_hp = get_meta("ai_hp") if has_meta("ai_hp") else 100
		ai_hp -= damage
		if ai_hp < 0: ai_hp = 0
		
		set_meta("ai_hp", ai_hp)
		get_node("UI/HUD/InfoBar/H2/HP").text = str(ai_hp)
		var ai_money = get_meta("ai_money") if has_meta("ai_money") else 500
		print("[PlayerContainer] 💰 AI деньги: ", ai_money, " | HP: ", ai_hp)
		
		if ai_hp <= 0:
			print("[PlayerContainer] ❌ AI проиграл!")
			
	else:
		# === ИГРОК КОНТЕЙНЕР ===
		GameSession.spend_base_health(damage)
		get_node("UI/HUD/InfoBar/H2/HP").text = str(GameSession.base_health)
		if has_node("UI/HUD/InfoBar/H/Money"):
			get_node("UI/HUD/InfoBar/H/Money").text = str(int(GameSession.current_money_in_game_session))
		
		if GameSession.base_health <= 0:
			print("[PlayerContainer] ❌ Игрок проиграл!")
			
			# Вызываем конец игры
			if game_end_controller:
				if GameSession.game_mode != GameConstants.GameMode.CAMPAIGN:
					game_end_controller.end_game()
				else:
					game_end_controller.end_game_company()
					
func _find_opponent_container():
	var root = get_tree().current_scene
	if name == "Player1Container":
		var opponent = root.get_node("CanvasLayer/HBoxContainer/Player2Container")
		print("[PlayerContainer] Игрок → ищу AI контейнер: ", opponent.name if opponent else "NULL")
		return opponent
	elif name == "Player2Container":
		var opponent = root.get_node("CanvasLayer/HBoxContainer/Player1Container")
		print("[PlayerContainer] AI → ищу игрока контейнер: ", opponent.name if opponent else "NULL")
		return opponent
	else:
		print("[PlayerContainer] ⚠️ Неизвестный контейнер: ", name)
		return null
		
func on_signal_spawn_enemies(wave: Array, enemy_progress: float):
	if is_player and wave_controller:
		wave_controller.spawn_enemies(wave, enemy_progress)
	
func on_stone(number_block: int, duration: int):
	if not is_player: return
	var available_turrets = []
	var turret_layer = map_node.find_child("Turret", true, false)
	if not turret_layer: return
	for turret in turret_layer.get_children():
		if is_instance_valid(turret) and not turret.get_node("NinePatchRect").visible:
			available_turrets.append(turret)
	if available_turrets.is_empty(): return
	while number_block > 0 and not available_turrets.is_empty():
		var idx = randi() % available_turrets.size()
		available_turrets[idx].stone_effect_start(duration)
		available_turrets.remove_at(idx)
		number_block -= 1

func record_tower_damage(tower_type: String, amount: float):
	if not tower_damage_history.has(tower_type):
		tower_damage_history[tower_type] = 0.0
	tower_damage_history[tower_type] += amount

func record_enemy_kill(enemy_name: String):
	if not enemy_kill_history.has(enemy_name):
		enemy_kill_history[enemy_name] = 0
	enemy_kill_history[enemy_name] += 1
	
	var is_ai = get_meta("is_ai_player") if has_meta("is_ai_player") else false
	
	if is_ai:
		# AI контейнер — деньги уже обновлены в Enemy.gd
		var ai_money = get_meta("ai_money")
		print("[PlayerContainer] 💰 AI убил ", enemy_name, " | Баланс: ", ai_money)
	else:
		# Игрок контейнер — деньги в GameSession
		print("[PlayerContainer] 💰 Игрок убил ", enemy_name, " | Баланс: ", GameSession.current_money_in_game_session)
		
func _process(delta):
	if is_player:
		if build_controller and build_controller.build_mode:
			build_controller.update_tower_preview()
		
		if pvp_manager and Time.get_ticks_msec() % 200 < delta * 1000:
			var effects = get_local_effects()
			if effects.size() > 0:
				print("📦 [PLAYER] Отправляю эффекты: ", effects) # ЛОГ ОТПРАВКИ
			
			pvp_manager.send_game_state(
				GameSession.base_health,
				GameSession.current_money_in_game_session,
				GameSession.current_wave,
				get_local_towers(),
				get_local_enemies(),
				effects
			)

func get_local_towers() -> Array:
	var towers = []
	if not is_instance_valid(map_node): return towers
	var turret_layer = map_node.find_child("Turret", true, false)
	if not turret_layer: return towers
	
	max_damage_val = 0
	max_damage_tower_id = -1
	
	for turret in turret_layer.get_children():
		if is_instance_valid(turret):
			var t_data = {
				"type": turret.type, 
				"pos": [snapped(turret.position.x, 0.1), snapped(turret.position.y, 0.1)], 
				"lvl": turret.current_lvl,
				"ability_count": 0,
				"color": "",
				"debuff_icon": "" 
			}
			
			if "ability_0" in turret: t_data["ability_count"] = int(turret.ability_0)
			elif "accumulated_tokens" in turret: t_data["ability_count"] = int(turret.accumulated_tokens)
			if turret.has_node("Turret"): t_data["color"] = turret.get_node("Turret").modulate.to_html()
			
			if turret.has_node("NinePatchRect") and turret.get_node("NinePatchRect").visible:
				var tex = turret.get_node("NinePatchRect").texture
				if tex: t_data["debuff_icon"] = tex.resource_path
			
			if turret.inflicted > max_damage_val:
				max_damage_val = turret.inflicted
				max_damage_tower_id = turret.id
				
			towers.append(t_data)
	return towers

func get_local_enemies() -> Array:
	var enemies_data = []
	if not is_instance_valid(map_node): return enemies_data
	var path_node = map_node.find_child("Path", true, false)
	if not path_node: return enemies_data
	for path in path_node.get_children():
		for enemy in path.get_children():
			if is_instance_valid(enemy) and enemy.has_method("get_progress_ratio"):
				if not enemy.has_meta("network_id"):
					enemy.set_meta("network_id", str(enemy.get_instance_id()) + "_" + str(randi()))
				var max_h = enemy.base_hp if "base_hp" in enemy else enemy.hp
				var variant = 0
				if "ind" in enemy: variant = enemy.ind
				enemies_data.append({
					"net_id": enemy.get_meta("network_id"),
					"name": str(enemy.names),
					"hp": enemy.hp,
					"max_hp": max_h, 
					"progress": snapped(enemy.progress_ratio, 0.001),
					"variant": variant
				})
	return enemies_data

func get_local_effects() -> Array:
	var effects = []
	if not is_instance_valid(map_node): return effects
	
	# Ищем эффекты СКОРОСТИ
	# Важно: Используем get_tree().get_nodes_in_group("speed") и фильтруем по родителю
	# Это надежнее, чем find_children, если иерархия сложная
	var all_speeds = get_tree().get_nodes_in_group("speed")
	
	for child in all_speeds:
		if is_instance_valid(child):
			# Проверяем, принадлежит ли этот эффект НАШЕЙ карте (PlayerContainer)
			# Используем is_ancestor_of
			if map_node.is_ancestor_of(child) or child.get_parent() == map_node:
				var rel_pos = map_node.to_local(child.global_position)
				effects.append({
					"id": str(child.get_instance_id()),
					"type": "speed",
					"pos": [snapped(rel_pos.x, 1), snapped(rel_pos.y, 1)]
				})
	return effects

func update_money_ui():
	"""Обновляет отображение денег в UI контейнера"""
	if not is_player:
		# AI контейнер: деньги скрыты
		var money_label = get_node_or_null("UI/HUD/InfoBar/H/Money")
		if money_label:
			money_label.visible = false
		return
	
	# Игрок: обновляем деньги из GameSession
	if has_node("UI/HUD/InfoBar/H/Money"):
		get_node("UI/HUD/InfoBar/H/Money").text = str(int(GameSession.current_money_in_game_session))
		
func sync_opponent_state(state: Dictionary):
	if is_player: return

	var hp_label = get_node_or_null("UI/HUD/InfoBar/H2/HP")
	if hp_label: hp_label.text = str(int(state.get("hp", 100)))
	var money_label = get_node_or_null("UI/HUD/InfoBar/H/Money")
	if money_label: money_label.text = str(int(state.get("money", 500.0)))
	var wave_label = get_node_or_null("UI/HUD/InfoBar/H3/WaveValue")
	if wave_label: wave_label.text = str(int(state.get("wave", 0)) + 1)
	
	# --- БАШНИ ---
	if is_instance_valid(map_node):
		var turret_layer = map_node.find_child("Turret", true, false)
		if turret_layer:
			var current_tower_positions = []
			for tower_data in state.get("towers", []):
				var pos_vec = Vector2(tower_data.pos[0], tower_data.pos[1])
				current_tower_positions.append(pos_vec)
				if spectator_towers_map.has(pos_vec) and is_instance_valid(spectator_towers_map[pos_vec]):
					var t = spectator_towers_map[pos_vec]
					if t.type != tower_data.type:
						t.queue_free()
						_create_spectator_tower(tower_data, pos_vec, turret_layer)
					else:
						if t.current_lvl != tower_data.lvl: t.current_lvl = tower_data.lvl
						_update_tower_visuals(t, tower_data)
				else:
					_create_spectator_tower(tower_data, pos_vec, turret_layer)
			var pos_to_remove = []
			for pos in spectator_towers_map.keys():
				if not pos in current_tower_positions:
					var t = spectator_towers_map[pos]
					if is_instance_valid(t): t.queue_free()
					pos_to_remove.append(pos)
			for pos in pos_to_remove: spectator_towers_map.erase(pos)
	
	# --- ВРАГИ (ИСПРАВЛЕНО) ---
	if is_instance_valid(map_node):
		var path_node = map_node.find_child("Path", true, false)
		if not path_node: return
		var current_packet_ids = []
		
		for enemy_data in state.get("enemies", []):
			var net_id = str(enemy_data.get("net_id", "unknown"))
			current_packet_ids.append(net_id)
			
			if spectator_enemies.has(net_id) and is_instance_valid(spectator_enemies[net_id]):
				# ОБНОВЛЕНИЕ СУЩЕСТВУЮЩЕГО
				var enemy = spectator_enemies[net_id]
				if not enemy.is_inside_tree(): continue
				
				if "server_progress_ratio" in enemy:
					enemy.server_progress_ratio = float(enemy_data.progress)
				else:
					enemy.progress_ratio = float(enemy_data.progress)
					
				enemy.hp = float(enemy_data.hp)
				_update_enemy_visuals(enemy, enemy_data)
			else:
				# СОЗДАНИЕ НОВОГО
				var enemy_name = str(enemy_data.name)
				var enemy_scene_path = _get_enemy_scene_path(enemy_name)
				
				# --- ФИКС: УБРАЛИ FileAccess.file_exists, сразу грузим ---
				var enemy_scene = load(enemy_scene_path)
				
				if enemy_scene:
					var enemy = enemy_scene.instantiate()
					enemy.hp = float(enemy_data.hp)
					enemy.base_hp = float(enemy_data.get("max_hp", enemy.hp))
					
					if "11" in enemy_name and enemy_data.has("variant"):
						var variant = int(enemy_data.variant) + 1
						if variant > 0 and variant <= 3:
							# Тут тоже используем load напрямую
							var tex_path = "res://Assets/Props/enemy_11_" + str(variant) + ".png"
							var tex = load(tex_path)
							if tex: enemy.get_node("Sprite2D").texture = tex
					
					_set_spectator_speed(enemy, enemy_name)
					
					if "server_progress_ratio" in enemy:
						enemy.server_progress_ratio = float(enemy_data.progress)
					
					if path_node.is_inside_tree():
						path_node.get_child(0).add_child(enemy)
						enemy.progress_ratio = float(enemy_data.progress)
						spectator_enemies[net_id] = enemy
						_update_enemy_visuals(enemy, enemy_data)
				else:
					# Логируем ошибку, если файла реально нет в APK
					print("❌ [SPECTATOR] Ошибка загрузки сцены врага: ", enemy_scene_path)
		
		var ids_to_remove = []
		for net_id in spectator_enemies.keys():
			if not net_id in current_packet_ids:
				var enemy = spectator_enemies[net_id]
				if is_instance_valid(enemy): enemy.queue_free()
				ids_to_remove.append(net_id)
		for id in ids_to_remove: spectator_enemies.erase(id)

	# --- ЭФФЕКТЫ ---
	if is_instance_valid(map_node) and state.has("effects"):
		var current_effect_ids = []
		for eff_data in state.get("effects", []):
			var eid = str(eff_data.id)
			current_effect_ids.append(eid)
			
			if not spectator_effects.has(eid):
				var obj = null
				if eff_data.type == "speed":
					obj = load("res://Scenes/SupportScenes/road_speed.tscn").instantiate()
				
				if obj:
					obj.position = Vector2(eff_data.pos[0], eff_data.pos[1])
					obj.z_index = 0 
					map_node.add_child(obj)
					spectator_effects[eid] = obj
		
		var eff_to_remove = []
		for eid in spectator_effects.keys():
			if not eid in current_effect_ids:
				var obj = spectator_effects[eid]
				if is_instance_valid(obj): obj.queue_free()
				eff_to_remove.append(eid)
		for eid in eff_to_remove: spectator_effects.erase(eid)
		
func _create_spectator_tower(tower_data, pos, parent):
	var turret = TurretFactory.create_turret(tower_data.type, pos)
	if turret:
		# ← КРИТИЧЕСКИ ВАЖНЫЙ ПОРЯДОК:
		# 1. Сначала устанавливаем is_opponent
		turret.is_opponent = true
		
		# 2. Потом устанавливаем параметры
		turret.current_lvl = tower_data.lvl
		
		# 3. Добавляем в дерево
		parent.add_child(turret)
		
		# 4. ТОЛЬКО ПОСЛЕ add_child устанавливаем built!
		turret.built = true
		
		# 5. Вручную вызываем _ready (если нужно)
		if turret.has_method("_ready"):
			turret._ready()
		
		spectator_towers_map[pos] = turret
		_update_tower_visuals(turret, tower_data)
		
func _update_enemy_visuals(enemy, data):
	if enemy.has_node("HealthBar"):
		var bar = enemy.get_node("HealthBar")
		bar.visible = true
		bar.top_level = true 
		bar.max_value = float(data.get("max_hp", enemy.base_hp))
		bar.value = float(enemy.hp)
		bar.global_position = enemy.global_position + Vector2(-30, -40)
		bar.rotation = 0

func _update_tower_visuals(turret, data):
	var count = int(data.get("ability_count", 0))
	if count > 0:
		if turret.has_node("Panel"):
			turret.get_node("Panel").visible = true
			if turret.get_node("Panel").has_node("Label"):
				turret.get_node("Panel/Label").text = str(count)
		if turret.has_node("TokenLabel"):
			turret.get_node("TokenLabel").visible = true
			if turret.get_node("TokenLabel").has_node("Label"):
				turret.get_node("TokenLabel/Label").text = str(count)
	else:
		if turret.has_node("Panel"): turret.get_node("Panel").visible = false
		if turret.has_node("TokenLabel"): turret.get_node("TokenLabel").visible = false
	
	if data.has("color") and data["color"] != "" and turret.has_node("Turret"):
		turret.get_node("Turret").modulate = Color(data["color"])
		
	var icon_path = data.get("debuff_icon", "")
	if icon_path != "":
		if turret.has_node("NinePatchRect"):
			turret.get_node("NinePatchRect").visible = true
			turret.get_node("NinePatchRect").texture = load(icon_path)
	else:
		if turret.has_node("NinePatchRect"):
			turret.get_node("NinePatchRect").visible = false

func _get_enemy_scene_path(enemy_name: String) -> String:
	if enemy_name.begins_with("Enemy_"):
		return "res://Scenes/Enemies/%s.tscn" % enemy_name
	var boss_id = 0
	if enemy_name.begins_with("BOSS_"): boss_id = int(enemy_name.split("_")[1])
	else: boss_id = int(float(enemy_name))
	return "res://Scenes/EnemiesBoss/Enemy_boss_%d.tscn" % boss_id

func _set_spectator_speed(enemy, enemy_name):
	var id = 0
	if enemy_name.begins_with("Enemy_"):
		var parts = enemy_name.split("_")
		if parts.size() > 1: id = int(parts[1]) - 1
		enemy.speed = GameConstants.DATA_ENEMY.get(id, {}).get("speed", 100)
	else:
		if enemy_name.begins_with("BOSS_"):
			id = int(enemy_name.split("_")[1])
		else:
			id = int(float(enemy_name))
			
		if GameConstants.DATA_ENEMY_BOSS.has(id):
			enemy.speed = GameConstants.DATA_ENEMY_BOSS[id].get("speed", 100)
		else:
			enemy.speed = 100
			
	enemy.current_speed = enemy.speed


# Scenes\UI\profile_screen.gd
# Scenes/UI/ProfileScreen.gd
extends Control

@onready var back_button: Button = $BackButton
@onready var avatar_frame: TextureRect = $VScrollBar/VBoxContainer/Header/AvatarContainer/AvatarFrame
@onready var btn_change_avatar: Button = $VScrollBar/VBoxContainer/Header/AvatarContainer/BtnChangeAvatar
@onready var nickname_label: Label = $VScrollBar/VBoxContainer/Header/NicknameContainer/NicknameLabel
@onready var btn_edit_nickname: Button = $VScrollBar/VBoxContainer/Header/NicknameContainer/BtnEditNickname
@onready var friend_code_label: Label = $VScrollBar/VBoxContainer/Header/InfoContainer/FriendCodeLabel
@onready var btn_copy_code: Button = $VScrollBar/VBoxContainer/Header/InfoContainer/BtnCopyCode
@onready var title_label: Label = $VScrollBar/VBoxContainer/TitleLabel
@onready var stats_container: GridContainer = $VScrollBar/VBoxContainer/StatsContainer
@onready var titles_list: ItemList = $VScrollBar/VBoxContainer/TitlesList
@onready var deck_slots: Array[TextureRect] = [
	$VScrollBar/VBoxContainer/DeckContainer/DeckSlot1,
	$VScrollBar/VBoxContainer/DeckContainer/DeckSlot2,
	$VScrollBar/VBoxContainer/DeckContainer/DeckSlot3,
	$VScrollBar/VBoxContainer/DeckContainer/DeckSlot4
]

func _ready() -> void:
	_connect_signals()
	await _load_profile_data()

func _connect_signals() -> void:
	back_button.pressed.connect(_on_back_pressed)
	btn_change_avatar.pressed.connect(_on_change_avatar_pressed)
	btn_edit_nickname.pressed.connect(_on_edit_nickname_pressed)
	btn_copy_code.pressed.connect(_on_copy_code_pressed)
	
	if ProfileManager:
		ProfileManager.nickname_changed.connect(_on_profile_updated)
		ProfileManager.avatar_changed.connect(_on_profile_updated)
		ProfileManager.gold_changed.connect(_on_profile_updated)
	
	if AuthService:
		AuthService.auth_completed.connect(_on_auth_completed)

func _on_auth_completed(success: bool, user_data: Dictionary):
	if success:
		await _load_profile_data()

func _load_profile_data() -> void:
	var profile = _get_profile_data()
	
	if profile.is_empty():
		print("[ProfileScreen] ❌ Профиль пуст!")
		return
	
	# === НИКНЕЙМ — ПРАВИЛЬНОЕ ПОЛУЧЕНИЕ ===
	var nickname = profile.get("nickname", "Guest")
	if nickname is Dictionary:
		nickname = str(nickname.get("stringValue", "Guest"))
	nickname_label.text = str(nickname)
	print("[ProfileScreen] Никнейм: ", nickname_label.text)
	
	# === КОД ДРУГА ===
	var friend_code = profile.get("friend_code", "#00000000")
	if friend_code is Dictionary:
		friend_code = str(friend_code.get("stringValue", "#00000000"))
	friend_code_label.text = str(friend_code)
	
	# === АВАТАР ===
	var avatar_id = profile.get("avatar_id", 1)
	if avatar_id is Dictionary:
		avatar_id = int(avatar_id.get("integerValue", 1))
	var avatar_path = FirebaseHelper.get_avatar_resource_path(avatar_id)
	if avatar_path and FileAccess.file_exists(avatar_path):
		avatar_frame.texture = load(avatar_path)
	
	# === ТИТУЛ ===
	var titles = profile.get("titles", [])
	if titles.size() > 0:
		var first_title = titles[0]
		if first_title is Dictionary and first_title.has("name"):
			title_label.text = str(first_title["name"])
			title_label.visible = true
		else:
			title_label.visible = false
	else:
		title_label.visible = false
	
	# === СТАТИСТИКА ===
	var stats = profile.get("stats", {})
	_update_stats(stats)
	
	# === ЗОЛОТО — ПРАВИЛЬНОЕ ПОЛУЧЕНИЕ ===
	var gold = profile.get("gold", 0)
	if gold is Dictionary:
		gold = int(gold.get("integerValue", 0))
	print("[ProfileScreen] Золото: ", gold)
	
	# === СПИСОК ТИТУЛОВ ===
	_update_titles_list(titles)
	
	# === КОЛОДА ===
	_update_deck(profile.get("current_deck", [0, 1, 2, 3]))
	print("[ProfileScreen] Профиль загружен успешно!")

func _get_profile_data() -> Dictionary:
	"""Получает данные профиля из AuthService или ProfileManager"""
	if AuthService and AuthService.is_authenticated:
		var auth_profile = AuthService.get_profile_data()
		if not auth_profile.is_empty():
			return auth_profile
	
	if ProfileManager and not ProfileManager.profile_data.is_empty():
		return ProfileManager.profile_data
	
	return {}

func _update_stats(stats: Dictionary) -> void:
	if not is_instance_valid(stats_container):
		return
	
	# Проверяем что узлы существуют
	stats_container.get_node("Stat1").text = tr("KEY_RATING") + "1000"
	stats_container.get_node("Stat2").text = tr("KEY_VICTORY_1") + str(_get_int_value(stats.get("pvp_wins", 0)))
	stats_container.get_node("Stat3").text = tr("KEY_DEFEAT") + str(_get_int_value(stats.get("pvp_losses", 0)))
	stats_container.get_node("Stat4").text = tr("KEY_BEST_WAVE") + str(_get_int_value(stats.get("best_wave", 0)))
	
	# === ЗОЛОТО ИЗ ПРОФИЛЯ ===
	var gold = 0
	var profile = _get_profile_data()
	if not profile.is_empty():
		gold = profile.get("gold", 0)
		if gold is Dictionary:
			gold = int(gold.get("integerValue", 0))
	
	stats_container.get_node("Stat5").text = tr("KEY_GOLD") + str(gold)
	stats_container.get_node("Stat6").text = tr("KEY_TOWERS") + str(_get_int_value(stats.get("towers_built", 0)))

# === ВСПОМОГАТЕЛЬНАЯ ФУНКЦИЯ ДЛЯ КОНВЕРТАЦИИ ===
func _get_int_value(value) -> int:
	if value is int:
		return value
	elif value is Dictionary:
		if value.has("integerValue"):
			return int(value["integerValue"])
		elif value.has("doubleValue"):
			return int(value["doubleValue"])
	return 0

func _update_titles_list(titles: Array) -> void:
	if not is_instance_valid(titles_list):
		return
	
	titles_list.clear()
	
	for title in titles:
		var title_name = "Unknown"
		if title is Dictionary:
			title_name = title.get("name", "Unknown")
			if title_name is Dictionary:
				title_name = str(title_name.get("stringValue", "Unknown"))
		
		var earned_at = title.get("earned_at", 0)
		if earned_at is Dictionary:
			earned_at = int(earned_at.get("integerValue", 0))
		
		var date_str = _format_timestamp(earned_at)
		titles_list.add_item(str(title_name) + " (" + date_str + ")")

func _update_deck(deck: Array) -> void:
	for i in range(4):
		if i < deck.size() and i < deck_slots.size():
			var tower_id = deck[i]
			if tower_id is Dictionary:
				tower_id = int(tower_id.get("integerValue", i))
			
			var icon_path = "res://Assets/Props/towerDefense_tile_turret_%d.png" % (tower_id + 1)
			if FileAccess.file_exists(icon_path):
				deck_slots[i].texture = load(icon_path)

func _format_timestamp(timestamp: int) -> String:
	if timestamp == 0:
		return "N/A"
	var datetime = Time.get_datetime_dict_from_unix_time(timestamp)
	return "%04d-%02d-%02d" % [datetime.year, datetime.month, datetime.day]

func _on_back_pressed() -> void:
	get_tree().change_scene_to_file("res://Scenes/UI/Menu.tscn")

func _on_change_avatar_pressed() -> void:
	var avatar_dialog = load("res://Scenes/UI/AvatarSelectionDialog.tscn").instantiate()
	add_child(avatar_dialog)

func _on_edit_nickname_pressed() -> void:
	var nickname_dialog = load("res://Scenes/UI/NicknameDialog.tscn").instantiate()
	add_child(nickname_dialog)

func _on_copy_code_pressed() -> void:
	DisplayServer.clipboard_set(friend_code_label.text)
	print("Код скопирован: ", friend_code_label.text)

func _on_profile_updated() -> void:
	await _load_profile_data()


# Scenes\UI\pvp_ai_scene.gd
# Scenes/UI/pvp_ai_scene.gd
extends Node2D

@onready var player_container = $CanvasLayer/HBoxContainer/Player1Container
@onready var ai_container = $CanvasLayer/HBoxContainer/Player2Container
@onready var canvas_layer = $CanvasLayer

var ai_controller: AIPlayerController
var game_started := false
var _initializing := false  # Защита от двойного запуска

func _ready():
	# Защита от двойного вызова
	if _initializing:
		return
	_initializing = true
	
	print("[PvP AI] Инициализация...")
	
	# Устанавливаем режим первым
	GameSession.game_mode = GameConstants.GameMode.PVP_AI
	
	player_container.set_as_player(null)
	ai_container.set_as_ai_player()
	
	_show_status("Запуск AI сервера...")
	
	var success = await AIManager.start_ai_server()
	
	if not success:
		_show_status("❌ Ошибка запуска AI")
		await get_tree().create_timer(3.0).timeout
		_return_to_menu()
		return
	
	_show_status("AI готов. Старт через 2 сек...")
	await get_tree().create_timer(2.0).timeout
	
	_start_game()

func _exit_tree():
	# Гарантируем остановку при любом выходе
	if ai_controller:
		ai_controller.stop()
	AIManager.stop_ai_server()
	
func _start_game():
	game_started = true
	await AIManager.reset_memory()
	
	var waves = WaveGenerator.generate_default_waves({}).level_0
	
	# Запускаем волны
	player_container.start_game(waves)
	ai_container.start_game(waves)
	
	if player_container.wave_controller:
		player_container.wave_controller.start_next_wave()
	
	if ai_container.wave_controller:
		ai_container.wave_controller.start_next_wave()
	
	if canvas_layer.has_node("StatusPanel"):
		canvas_layer.get_node("StatusPanel").queue_free()
	
	# === ИНИЦИАЛИЗАЦИЯ HP В UI ===
	# Устанавливаем начальные HP противников
	player_container.update_opponent_hp(100)  # Игрок видит HP AI = 100
	ai_container.update_opponent_hp(100)      # AI видит HP игрока = 100
	
	print("[PvP AI] 🚀 HP инициализированы:")
	print("  Player1 видит HP врага: 100")
	print("  Player2 видит HP врага: 100")
	
	# Запускаем AI контроллер
	ai_controller = AIPlayerController.new()
	ai_container.add_child(ai_controller)
	ai_controller.setup(ai_container)
	
	ai_controller.action_decided.connect(_on_ai_action)
	
	print("[PvP AI] 🚀 Игра началась!")
	print("[PvP AI] Игрок: деньги видны, HP виден")
	print("[PvP AI] AI: деньги скрыты, HP виден")
	
func _on_ai_action(action: Dictionary):
	print("[PvP AI] AI действие: ", action)

func _process(_delta):
	if not game_started:
		return
	
	# Проверка окончания игры
	var player_hp = GameSession.base_health
	var ai_hp = ai_container.get_meta("ai_hp") if ai_container.has_meta("ai_hp") else 100
	
	if player_hp <= 0:
		_end_game(false)  # Игрок проиграл
	elif ai_hp <= 0:
		_end_game(true)   # Игрок выиграл

func _end_game(player_won: bool):
	if get_tree().paused:
		return
	
	game_started = false
	get_tree().paused = true
	
	# Останавливаем AI
	if ai_controller:
		ai_controller.stop()
	
	# Останавливаем сервер
	AIManager.stop_ai_server()
	
	# Показываем экран окончания
	_show_end_screen(player_won)

func _show_end_screen(is_win: bool):
	var panel = Panel.new()
	panel.name = "EndGameOverlay"
	panel.set_anchors_preset(Control.PRESET_FULL_RECT)
	panel.z_index = 100
	
	var style = StyleBoxFlat.new()
	style.bg_color = Color(0, 0, 0, 0.95)
	panel.add_theme_stylebox_override("panel", style)
	
	canvas_layer.add_child(panel)
	
	var center = CenterContainer.new()
	center.set_anchors_preset(Control.PRESET_FULL_RECT)
	panel.add_child(center)
	
	var vbox = VBoxContainer.new()
	vbox.custom_minimum_size = Vector2(800, 600)
	vbox.alignment = BoxContainer.ALIGNMENT_CENTER
	vbox.add_theme_constant_override("separation", 40)
	center.add_child(vbox)
	
	# Заголовок
	var label = Label.new()
	label.text = "ПОБЕДА!" if is_win else "ПОРАЖЕНИЕ"
	label.add_theme_font_size_override("font_size", 96)
	label.add_theme_color_override("font_color", Color.GREEN if is_win else Color.RED)
	label.horizontal_alignment = HORIZONTAL_ALIGNMENT_CENTER
	vbox.add_child(label)
	
	# Подзаголовок
	var subtitle = Label.new()
	subtitle.text = "Вы обыграли AI!" if is_win else "AI победил"
	subtitle.add_theme_font_size_override("font_size", 48)
	subtitle.horizontal_alignment = HORIZONTAL_ALIGNMENT_CENTER
	vbox.add_child(subtitle)
	
	# Статистика
	var stats = _get_towers_damage_stats()
	var stats_label = Label.new()
	stats_label.text = "MVP башня: " + stats[0].name if stats.size() > 0 else "Нет башен"
	stats_label.add_theme_font_size_override("font_size", 32)
	stats_label.horizontal_alignment = HORIZONTAL_ALIGNMENT_CENTER
	vbox.add_child(stats_label)
	
	# Кнопка выхода
	var btn = Button.new()
	btn.text = "В ГЛАВНОЕ МЕНЮ"
	btn.custom_minimum_size = Vector2(300, 60)
	btn.add_theme_font_size_override("font_size", 32)
	btn.process_mode = Node.PROCESS_MODE_ALWAYS
	btn.pressed.connect(_return_to_menu)
	vbox.add_child(btn)

func _get_towers_damage_stats() -> Array:
	var stats = []
	var damage_history = player_container.tower_damage_history
	
	for key in damage_history.keys():
		var parts = key.split("_")
		if parts.size() > 1:
			var id_part = parts[1].split("T")[0]
			var id = int(id_part)
			var t_name = tr("KEY_NAME_TURRET_" + str(id))
			stats.append({"name": t_name, "damage": damage_history[key]})
	
	stats.sort_custom(func(a, b): return a.damage > b.damage)
	return stats

func _show_status(text: String):
	if not canvas_layer.has_node("StatusPanel"):
		var l = Label.new()
		l.name = "StatusPanel"
		l.set_anchors_preset(Control.PRESET_CENTER)
		l.add_theme_font_size_override("font_size", 64)
		l.add_theme_color_override("font_shadow_color", Color.BLACK)
		canvas_layer.add_child(l)
	
	canvas_layer.get_node("StatusPanel").text = text

func _return_to_menu():
	get_tree().paused = false
	AIManager.stop_ai_server()  # Явно останавливаем перед сменой сцены
	
	await get_tree().create_timer(0.5).timeout 
	if OS.get_name() == "Android" or OS.get_name() == "iOS":
		get_tree().change_scene_to_file("res://Scenes/Mobile/Menu_mobile.tscn")
	else:
		get_tree().change_scene_to_file("res://Scenes/UI/Menu.tscn")


# Scenes\UI\pvp_game_scene.gd
# Scenes/UI/pvp_game_scene.gd
extends Node2D

@onready var my_container = $CanvasLayer/HBoxContainer/Player1Container
@onready var opponent_container = $CanvasLayer/HBoxContainer/Player2Container
@onready var canvas_layer = $CanvasLayer

var pvp_manager: Node
var waves_started := false
var game_start_time = 0

func _ready():
	pvp_manager = NetworkManager 
	print("PVP SCENE READY. PvPSession ID: ", PvPSession.player_id)
	
	my_container.set_as_player(NetworkManager)
	opponent_container.set_as_ai_player()
	
	if not NetworkManager.game_state_updated.is_connected(_on_game_state_updated):
		NetworkManager.game_state_updated.connect(_on_game_state_updated)
	if not NetworkManager.game_started.is_connected(_on_game_started):
		NetworkManager.game_started.connect(_on_game_started)
	if not NetworkManager.game_finished.is_connected(_on_game_finished):
		NetworkManager.game_finished.connect(_on_game_finished)
	
	NetworkManager.set_ready()

func _on_game_started(wave_data: Array):
	if waves_started: return
	waves_started = true
	game_start_time = Time.get_ticks_msec()
	if canvas_layer.has_node("StatusPanel"):
		canvas_layer.get_node("StatusPanel").queue_free()
	my_container.start_game(wave_data)
	opponent_container.start_game(wave_data)

func _on_game_state_updated(state: Dictionary):
	if state.has("winner") and state.winner:
		_on_game_finished(state.winner)
		return
	var opponent_data = {}
	if PvPSession.player_index == 0: opponent_data = state.get("p2_state", {})
	else: opponent_data = state.get("p1_state", {})
	if state.get("type") == "opponent_state": opponent_data = state
	if not opponent_data.is_empty():
		opponent_container.sync_opponent_state(opponent_data)

func _on_game_finished(winner_id: String):
	if get_tree().paused: return
	get_tree().paused = true
	var my_id = str(PvPSession.player_id)
	var win_id = str(winner_id)
	var is_win = (my_id == win_id)
	_show_end_game_screen(is_win)

func _show_end_game_screen(is_win: bool):
	var panel = Panel.new()
	panel.name = "EndGameOverlay"
	panel.set_anchors_preset(Control.PRESET_FULL_RECT)
	panel.z_index = 100 
	
	var style = StyleBoxFlat.new()
	style.bg_color = Color(0, 0, 0, 0.95)
	panel.add_theme_stylebox_override("panel", style)
	
	canvas_layer.add_child(panel)
	
	var center = CenterContainer.new()
	center.set_anchors_preset(Control.PRESET_FULL_RECT)
	panel.add_child(center)
	
	var vbox = VBoxContainer.new()
	vbox.custom_minimum_size = Vector2(1200, 1200)
	vbox.alignment = BoxContainer.ALIGNMENT_CENTER
	vbox.add_theme_constant_override("separation", 30)
	center.add_child(vbox)
	
	# Заголовок
	var label = Label.new()
	label.text = "ПОБЕДА!" if is_win else "ПОРАЖЕНИЕ"
	label.add_theme_font_size_override("font_size", 144)
	label.add_theme_color_override("font_color", Color.GREEN if is_win else Color.RED)
	label.horizontal_alignment = HORIZONTAL_ALIGNMENT_CENTER
	vbox.add_child(label)
	
	# Контейнер для статистики (одна колонка - Башни)
	var stats_container = VBoxContainer.new()
	stats_container.size_flags_horizontal = Control.SIZE_EXPAND_FILL
	vbox.add_child(stats_container)
	
	var stats_title = Label.new()
	stats_title.text = "Статистика башен (MVP):"
	stats_title.add_theme_font_size_override("font_size", 56)
	stats_title.horizontal_alignment = HORIZONTAL_ALIGNMENT_CENTER
	stats_container.add_child(stats_title)
	
	var towers_stats = _get_towers_damage_stats()
	var max_dmg = 1.0
	var total_dmg = 0.0
	for t in towers_stats: 
		total_dmg += t.damage
		if float(t.damage) > max_dmg: max_dmg = float(t.damage)
	
	var scroll = ScrollContainer.new()
	scroll.custom_minimum_size = Vector2(0, 300)
	scroll.size_flags_vertical = Control.SIZE_EXPAND_FILL
	stats_container.add_child(scroll)
	
	var list_vbox = VBoxContainer.new()
	list_vbox.size_flags_horizontal = Control.SIZE_EXPAND_FILL
	scroll.add_child(list_vbox)
	
	# Заголовки таблицы
	var header = HBoxContainer.new()
	list_vbox.add_child(header)
	_add_header_label(header, "Башня", 200)
	_add_header_label(header, "%", 80)
	_add_header_label(header, "Урон", 150)
	
	towers_stats.sort_custom(func(a, b): return a.damage > b.damage)
	
	for i in range(towers_stats.size()):
		var t = towers_stats[i]
		if t.damage <= 0: continue
		
		var row = HBoxContainer.new()
		list_vbox.add_child(row)
		
		var name_lbl = Label.new()
		name_lbl.text = t.name
		name_lbl.custom_minimum_size = Vector2(200, 0)
		name_lbl.clip_text = true
		row.add_child(name_lbl)
		
		var percent = 0.0
		if total_dmg > 0: percent = (t.damage / total_dmg) * 100.0
		var perc_lbl = Label.new()
		perc_lbl.text = "%.1f%%" % percent
		perc_lbl.custom_minimum_size = Vector2(80, 0)
		perc_lbl.horizontal_alignment = HORIZONTAL_ALIGNMENT_RIGHT
		row.add_child(perc_lbl)
		
		var val_lbl = Label.new()
		val_lbl.text = _format_number(int(t.damage))
		val_lbl.custom_minimum_size = Vector2(150, 0)
		val_lbl.horizontal_alignment = HORIZONTAL_ALIGNMENT_RIGHT
		row.add_child(val_lbl)
		
		var bar = ProgressBar.new()
		bar.custom_minimum_size = Vector2(100, 20)
		bar.size_flags_vertical = Control.SIZE_SHRINK_CENTER
		bar.show_percentage = false
		bar.max_value = max_dmg
		bar.value = t.damage
		
		# Цвета для ТОП-3
		var style_box = StyleBoxFlat.new()
		style_box.set_corner_radius_all(4)
		if i == 0: style_box.bg_color = Color.GOLD
		elif i == 1: style_box.bg_color = Color.SILVER
		elif i == 2: style_box.bg_color = Color(0.8, 0.5, 0.2)
		else: style_box.bg_color = Color.GRAY
		bar.add_theme_stylebox_override("fill", style_box)
		row.add_child(bar)

	# Кнопка в меню
	var btn_menu = Button.new()
	btn_menu.text = "В ГЛАВНОЕ МЕНЮ"
	btn_menu.custom_minimum_size = Vector2(300, 60)
	btn_menu.add_theme_font_size_override("font_size", 48)
	btn_menu.process_mode = Node.PROCESS_MODE_ALWAYS 
	btn_menu.pressed.connect(_on_exit_pressed)
	vbox.add_child(btn_menu)

func _add_header_label(parent, text, width):
	var l = Label.new()
	l.text = text
	l.custom_minimum_size = Vector2(width, 0)
	l.add_theme_color_override("font_color", Color.LIGHT_GRAY)
	parent.add_child(l)

func _get_towers_damage_stats() -> Array:
	var stats = []
	var damage_history = my_container.tower_damage_history
	
	for key in damage_history.keys():
		var parts = key.split("_")
		if parts.size() > 1:
			var id_part = parts[1].split("T")[0]
			var id = int(id_part)
			var t_name = tr("KEY_NAME_TURRET_" + str(id))
			stats.append({"name": t_name, "damage": damage_history[key]})
		
	return stats

func _format_number(n: int) -> String:
	var s = str(n)
	var res = ""
	var count = 0
	for i in range(s.length() - 1, -1, -1):
		if count > 0 and count % 3 == 0: res = " " + res
		res = s[i] + res
		count += 1
	return res

func _on_exit_pressed():
	get_tree().paused = false 
	PvPSession.room_id = ""
	if OS.get_name() == "Android" or OS.get_name() == "iOS":
		get_tree().change_scene_to_file("res://Scenes/Mobile/Menu_mobile.tscn")
	else:
		get_tree().change_scene_to_file("res://Scenes/UI/Menu.tscn")

func _show_status_message(text: String):
	if not canvas_layer.has_node("StatusPanel"):
		var l = Label.new()
		l.name = "StatusPanel"
		l.set_anchors_preset(Control.PRESET_CENTER)
		l.add_theme_font_size_override("font_size", 80)
		l.add_theme_color_override("font_shadow_color", Color.BLACK)
		canvas_layer.add_child(l)
	canvas_layer.get_node("StatusPanel").text = text


# Scenes\UI\stats_panel.gd
# Scenes/UI/StatsPanel.gd
extends Panel

@onready var stats_list = $VBoxContainer/ScrollContainer/StatsList
@onready var close_button = $VBoxContainer/CloseButton

var game_scene = null 
func _ready():
	visible = false
	if close_button:
		close_button.pressed.connect(func(): visible = false)

func update_stats():
	if not stats_list:
		print("❌ ОШИБКА: StatsList не найден!")
		return
	
	# Очистка списка
	for child in stats_list.get_children():
		child.queue_free()
	
	# Получаем доступ к главной сцене (GameScene)
	var game = get_tree().current_scene
	if not game: 
		print("❌ ОШИБКА: Главная сцена не найдена!")
		return

	# 1. Время игры
	# Мы берем время с начала старта сцены
	var elapsed = Time.get_ticks_msec() / 1000.0 
	_add_stat(tr("KEY_STATS_TIME") + ":", "%02d:%02d" % [int(elapsed/60), int(elapsed)%60])
	
	# 2. Убийства
	_add_stat(tr("KEY_STATS_KILLS") + ":", str(GameSession.session_enemies_killed))
	
	# 3. Золото
	_add_stat(tr("KEY_STATS_GOLD") + ":", str(int(GameSession.current_money_in_game_session)))
	
	# 4. Расчет урона башен
	var total_damage = 0.0
	# Ищем узел с башнями. В твоем проекте это Map/Turret
	var turret_layer = game.get_node_or_null("Map/Turret")
	
	if turret_layer:
		var built_turrets = turret_layer.get_children()
		
		# Считаем общий урон для процентов
		for t in built_turrets:
			if "inflicted" in t: total_damage += t.inflicted
		
		_add_stat(tr("KEY_STATS_TOTAL_DAMAGE") + ":", str(int(total_damage)))
		stats_list.add_child(HSeparator.new())
		
		# Детализация по колоде (4 башни)
		if game.has_node("Controllers/UIController"):
			var active_ids = game.get_node("Controllers/UIController").list_activity_turret
			for t_id in active_ids:
				var t_name = tr("KEY_NAME_TURRET_" + str(t_id))
				var damage_by_type = 0.0
				var count = 0
				
				for t in built_turrets:
					if "id" in t and t.id == (t_id - 1):
						count += 1
						damage_by_type += t.inflicted
				
				var percent = 0
				if total_damage > 0:
					percent = int((damage_by_type / total_damage) * 100)
				
				_add_stat(t_name + " (x" + str(count) + ")", str(int(damage_by_type)) + " (" + str(percent) + "%)")
	else:
		_add_stat("Инфо:", "Постройте башни для статистики")

# Функция добавления строки
func _add_stat(label_text: String, value_text: String):
	var hbox = HBoxContainer.new()
	hbox.size_flags_horizontal = Control.SIZE_EXPAND_FILL
	
	var lbl = Label.new()
	lbl.text = label_text
	lbl.size_flags_horizontal = Control.SIZE_EXPAND_FILL
	lbl.add_theme_font_size_override("font_size", 20)
	var val = Label.new()
	val.text = value_text
	val.horizontal_alignment = HORIZONTAL_ALIGNMENT_RIGHT
	val.add_theme_font_size_override("font_size", 20)
	
	hbox.add_child(lbl)
	hbox.add_child(val)
	stats_list.add_child(hbox)


# Systems\AIManager.gd
# Systems/AIManager.gd
extends Node

signal ai_ready()
signal ai_action(action: Dictionary)
signal ai_error(message: String)

var tcp: StreamPeerTCP
var server_process: int = -1
var is_connected: bool = false
var is_server_ready: bool = false
var is_shutting_down: bool = false

const SERVER_PORT = 5555
const SERVER_HOST = "127.0.0.1"
const PYTHON_PATH = "python"
const USE_EXE = true

var _pending_requests: Array = []

func _ready():
	# Убиваем старые процессы при старте
	_kill_existing_servers()

func _kill_existing_servers():
	# Если уже есть запущенный процесс — убиваем
	if server_process != -1:
		print("[AIManager] Убиваем старый процесс PID: ", server_process)
		OS.kill(server_process)
		server_process = -1
		is_connected = false
		is_server_ready = false
		await get_tree().create_timer(1.0).timeout

func start_ai_server() -> bool:
	# Защита от двойного запуска
	if server_process != -1 and OS.is_process_running(server_process):
		print("[AIManager] Сервер уже запущен PID: ", server_process)
		if is_server_ready:
			return true
		# Пробуем подключиться к уже запущенному
		return await connect_to_server()
	
	# Убиваем зависший процесс если есть
	if server_process != -1:
		OS.kill(server_process)
		server_process = -1
		await get_tree().create_timer(0.5).timeout
	
	var server_path = ""
	var args = []
	
	if USE_EXE:
		server_path = _get_server_exe_path()
		
		if not FileAccess.file_exists(server_path):
			push_error("[AIManager] ai_server.exe не найден: ", server_path)
			return false
		
		args = []
	else:
		server_path = PYTHON_PATH
		var script_path = _get_server_script_path()
		
		if not FileAccess.file_exists(script_path):
			push_error("[AIManager] ai_server.py не найден: ", script_path)
			return false
		
		args = [ProjectSettings.globalize_path(script_path)]
	
	var global_path = ProjectSettings.globalize_path(server_path)
	
	print("[AIManager] Запуск: ", global_path)
	print("[AIManager] Args: ", args)
	
	# Запускаем процесс
	server_process = OS.create_process(global_path, args)
	
	if server_process == -1:
		push_error("[AIManager] Не удалось запустить процесс")
		emit_signal("ai_error", "Failed to start AI process")
		return false
	
	print("[AIManager] ✅ Процесс запущен (PID: ", server_process, ")")
	
	# Ждём запуска с проверкой живости
	for attempt in range(60):
		await get_tree().create_timer(1.0).timeout
		
		if is_shutting_down:
			print("[AIManager] Прерван из-за shutdown")
			return false
		
		# Проверяем жив ли процесс
		if not OS.is_process_running(server_process):
			push_error("[AIManager] ❌ Процесс завершился на секунде ", attempt + 1)
			server_process = -1
			return false
		
		print("[AIManager] Попытка подключения ", attempt + 1, "/60...")
		
		# Пробуем подключиться
		var test_tcp = StreamPeerTCP.new()
		if test_tcp.connect_to_host(SERVER_HOST, SERVER_PORT) == OK:
			await get_tree().create_timer(0.3).timeout
			test_tcp.poll()
			if test_tcp.get_status() == StreamPeerTCP.STATUS_CONNECTED:
				test_tcp.disconnect_from_host()
				print("[AIManager] ✅ Сервер готов на секунде ", attempt + 1)
				return await connect_to_server()
		
		test_tcp.disconnect_from_host()
	
	push_error("[AIManager] Сервер не запустился за 60 секунд")
	emit_signal("ai_error", "Server startup timeout")
	return false

func stop_ai_server():
	is_shutting_down = true
	
	# Закрываем соединение
	if tcp:
		tcp.disconnect_from_host()
		tcp = null
	
	# Убиваем процесс
	if server_process != -1:
		if OS.is_process_running(server_process):
			OS.kill(server_process)
			print("[AIManager] Сервер остановлен (PID: ", server_process, ")")
		server_process = -1
	
	is_connected = false
	is_server_ready = false
	is_shutting_down = false

func _get_server_exe_path() -> String:
	# Пробуем несколько вариантов расположения .exe
	var paths = [
		"res://addons/td_ai/ai_server.exe",
		"res://addons/td_ai/dist/ai_server.exe",
		"res://AI/ai_server.exe",
		"res://ai_server.exe"
	]
	
	for path in paths:
		if FileAccess.file_exists(path):
			return path
	
	return paths[0]  # Fallback

func _get_server_script_path() -> String:
	# Пробуем несколько вариантов пути
	var paths = [
		"res://addons/td_ai/ai_server.py",
		"res://AI/ai_server.py",
		"res://Scripts/AI/ai_server.py"
	]
	
	for path in paths:
		if FileAccess.file_exists(path):
			return path
	
	return paths[0]  # Возвращаем первый как fallback

func connect_to_server() -> bool:
	if is_connected and tcp and tcp.get_status() == StreamPeerTCP.STATUS_CONNECTED:
		print("[AIManager] Уже подключены")
		return true
	
	# Закрываем старое соединение
	if tcp:
		tcp.disconnect_from_host()
		tcp = null
	
	tcp = StreamPeerTCP.new()
	var err = tcp.connect_to_host(SERVER_HOST, SERVER_PORT)
	
	if err != OK:
		push_error("[AIManager] Ошибка connect_to_host: ", err)
		emit_signal("ai_error", "Connection failed")
		return false
	
	print("[AIManager] Подключение к ", SERVER_HOST, ":", SERVER_PORT, "...")
	
	# Ждём подключения (максимум 50 * 0.1 = 5 секунд)
	var max_attempts = 50
	for i in range(max_attempts):
		await get_tree().create_timer(0.1).timeout
		
		if is_shutting_down:
			print("[AIManager] Прерван из-за shutdown")
			return false
		
		tcp.poll()
		var status = tcp.get_status()
		
		if status == StreamPeerTCP.STATUS_CONNECTED:
			is_connected = true
			print("[AIManager] ✅ Подключено к AI серверу")
			
			# Проверяем доступность
			if await ping():
				is_server_ready = true
				print("[AIManager] 🚀 AI готов к работе")
				emit_signal("ai_ready")
				return true
			else:
				push_error("[AIManager] Сервер не отвечает на ping")
				emit_signal("ai_error", "Server ping failed")
				return false
		
		elif status == StreamPeerTCP.STATUS_ERROR:
			push_error("[AIManager] Ошибка подключения")
			emit_signal("ai_error", "Connection error")
			return false
	
	push_error("[AIManager] Таймаут подключения")
	emit_signal("ai_error", "Connection timeout")
	return false

func ping() -> bool:
	var request = {"command": "ping"}
	var response = await send_request(request)
	return response.has("status") and response.status == "ok"

func reset_memory() -> bool:
	if not is_server_ready:
		return false
	
	var request = {"command": "reset"}
	var response = await send_request(request)
	
	if response.has("status") and response.status == "ok":
		print("[AIManager] Память AI сброшена")
		return true
	
	return false

func predict(game_state: Dictionary) -> Dictionary:
	if not is_server_ready:
		push_warning("[AIManager] Сервер не готов")
		return {"error": "not_ready", "action_type": 0}
	
	# Подготовка данных для AI
	var ai_data = _prepare_ai_data(game_state)
	
	var request = {
		"command": "predict",
		"data": ai_data
	}
	
	var response = await send_request(request)
	
	if response.has("error"):
		push_error("[AIManager] Ошибка AI: ", response.error)
		return {"action_type": 0, "error": response.error}
	
	return response

func send_request(request: Dictionary) -> Dictionary:
	"""
	Отправляет запрос на AI сервер и получает ответ.
	Возвращает распарсенный JSON или ошибку.
	"""
	# === 1. ПРОВЕРКА СОЕДИНЕНИЯ ===
	if not is_connected or not tcp:
		push_error("[AIManager] ❌ Нет соединения с AI сервером")
		return {"error": "not_connected"}
	
	tcp.poll()
	var status = tcp.get_status()
	
	if status != StreamPeerTCP.STATUS_CONNECTED:
		push_error("[AIManager] ❌ Соединение потеряно (статус: ", status, ")")
		is_connected = false
		is_server_ready = false
		return {"error": "connection_lost"}
	
	# === 2. СЕРИАЛИЗАЦИЯ ЗАПРОСА ===
	var json_str = JSON.stringify(request) + "\n"  # ← ВАЖНО: \n в конце!
	var data = json_str.to_utf8_buffer()
	
	# === 3. ОТПРАВКА ===
	var err = tcp.put_data(data)
	if err != OK:
		push_error("[AIManager] ❌ Ошибка отправки: ", err)
		is_connected = false
		return {"error": "send_failed"}
	
	# === 4. ЧТЕНИЕ ОТВЕТА ===
	var response_text = ""
	var max_wait = 100  # 10 секунд (0.1 * 100)
	var received_complete = false
	
	for i in range(max_wait):
		# Проверка shutdown
		if is_shutting_down:
			print("[AIManager] ⚠️ Прервано из-за shutdown")
			return {"error": "shutdown"}
		
		await get_tree().create_timer(0.1).timeout
		
		tcp.poll()
		
		# Проверка соединения после poll
		if tcp.get_status() != StreamPeerTCP.STATUS_CONNECTED:
			print("[AIManager] ❌ Соединение потеряно во время чтения")
			is_connected = false
			is_server_ready = false
			return {"error": "connection_lost"}
		
		# Чтение доступных данных
		var available = tcp.get_available_bytes()
		if available > 0:
			var chunk = tcp.get_utf8_string(available)
			response_text += chunk
			
			# ← ВАЖНО: Ищем конец сообщения (\n)
			if "\n" in chunk:
				# Берём ТОЛЬКО первую строку (до \n)
				var lines = response_text.split("\n")
				response_text = lines[0].strip_edges()
				received_complete = true
				break
	
	# === 5. ПРОВЕРКА ТАЙМАУТА ===
	if not received_complete or response_text.is_empty():
		print("[AIManager] ⚠️ Таймаут чтения ответа (получено: ", response_text.length(), " символов)")
		return {"error": "timeout"}
	
	# === 6. ПАРСИНГ JSON ===
	var json = JSON.new()
	var parse_result = json.parse(response_text)
	
	if parse_result != OK:
		print("[AIManager] ❌ Невалидный JSON!")
		print("[AIManager] Error code: ", parse_result)
		print("[AIManager] Raw response: ", response_text.substr(0, 300))
		return {"error": "invalid_json", "raw": response_text}
	
	var result = json.data
